+tmp.cleanup()

+run('branch-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": []'))
+run('branch-no-such', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": "no-such"'))
+run('tip-empty', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": ""',t))
+run('tip-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": null',t))
+run('tip-uppercase', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "([0-9a-f]{40})"',lambda x:'"oracle_tip": "'+x.group(1).upper()+'"',t))
+run('tip-off-branch', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"',f'"oracle_tip": "{o}"',t))
+run('plan-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": ""'))
+run('plan-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": {}'))
+run('plan-absolute', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "/tmp/plan.md"'))
+run('plan-missing', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "no-such.md"'))
+run('plan-normalizing-dotdot', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "contracts/../plan-fixture.md"'))
+run('record-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run('row-semantic-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | changed |'), commit=True)
+run('row-trailing-space-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | the row |   '), commit=True)
+run('realized-map-on-superseded', lambda t,o:t+'\n```json\n{"realized_map":{"C1":"x","C2":"y"}}\n```\n')
+run('manifest-anchor', remove_packet=False)
+run('header-union-only', remove_packet=False, packet_mut=lambda t:t.replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"','"prose:header-c1"').replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", ','"prose:header-c2", ')+'\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1/C2\n')
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+(root/'packets'/'ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md').write_text('# old\n\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n',encoding='utf-8')
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print(f'grandfathered-header-union-only: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)) + f' (grandfathered={stats["grandfathered"]})')
+tmp.cleanup()

+import importlib.util
+from pathlib import Path
+
+LINTER = Path('/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py')
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('d8lint', LINTER)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+def run(name, draft_mut=lambda t, off: t, packet_mut=None, remove_packet=True, commit=False):
+    fx = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fx is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fx
+    draft.write_text(draft_mut(text, off), encoding='utf-8')
+    if remove_packet:
+        packet.unlink()
+    elif packet_mut:

+    if commit:
+        assert m._git_ok(root, 'add', '-A') and m._git_ok(root, 'commit', '-q', '-m', name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root/'packets', root/'contracts', m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f'{name}: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else f'RED[{len(errors)}] ' + ' || '.join(errors)))
+    tmp.cleanup()

+run('branch-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": []'))
+run('branch-no-such', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": "no-such"'))
+run('tip-empty', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": ""',t))
+run('tip-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": null',t))
+run('tip-uppercase', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "([0-9a-f]{40})"',lambda x:'"oracle_tip": "'+x.group(1).upper()+'"',t))
+run('tip-off-branch', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"',f'"oracle_tip": "{o}"',t))
+run('plan-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": ""'))
+run('plan-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": {}'))
+run('plan-absolute', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "/tmp/plan.md"'))
+run('plan-missing', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "no-such.md"'))
+run('plan-normalizing-dotdot', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "contracts/../plan-fixture.md"'))
+run('record-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run('row-semantic-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | changed |'), commit=True)
+run('row-trailing-space-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | the row |   '), commit=True)
+run('realized-map-on-superseded', lambda t,o:t+'\n```json\n{"realized_map":{"C1":"x","C2":"y"}}\n```\n')
+run('manifest-anchor', remove_packet=False)
+run('header-union-only', remove_packet=False, packet_mut=lambda t:t.replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"','"prose:header-c1"').replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", ','"prose:header-c2", ')+'\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1/C2\n')
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()

+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print(f'grandfathered-header-union-only: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)) + f' (grandfathered={stats["grandfathered"]})')
+tmp.cleanup()

    docs(v3): chapter-close carve-out — a superseded draft realizes through its successor
    
    User-ratified 2026-08-03 with the ch13 re-derivation P1: the
    draft-close condition now reads realized OR superseded, with a
    superseded draft's realization obligation transferring to its
    successor surface, which must itself stand realized at the close.
    Resolves the executor's STEP 3 flag: without this, ch13 would be
    unclosable by the letter of the rule once its contract flips to the
    terminal superseded status. Prose-enforced condition; no lint change.

  conditions:** ZERO

  close — never silently skipped — **plus the three draft-close
  conditions:** ZERO

  — naturally vacuous when no draft exists); EVERY chapter-referenced

  draft-metrics

+import importlib.util
+from pathlib import Path
+
+LINTER = Path('/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py')
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('d8lint', LINTER)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+def run(name, draft_mut=lambda t, off: t, packet_mut=None, remove_packet=True, commit=False):
+    fx = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fx is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fx
+    draft.write_text(draft_mut(text, off), encoding='utf-8')
+    if remove_packet:
+        packet.unlink()
+    elif packet_mut:
+        packet.write_text(packet_mut(packet.read_text(encoding='utf-8')), encoding='utf-8')
+    if commit:
+        assert m._git_ok(root, 'add', '-A') and m._git_ok(root, 'commit', '-q', '-m', name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root/'packets', root/'contracts', m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f'{name}: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else f'RED[{len(errors)}] ' + ' || '.join(errors)))
+    tmp.cleanup()

+LINTER = Path('/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py')
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('d8lint', LINTER)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+def run(name, draft_mut=lambda t, off: t, packet_mut=None, remove_packet=True, commit=False):
+    fx = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fx is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fx
+    draft.write_text(draft_mut(text, off), encoding='utf-8')
+    if remove_packet:
+        packet.unlink()
+    elif packet_mut:
+        packet.write_text(packet_mut(packet.read_text(encoding='utf-8')), encoding='utf-8')
+    if commit:
+        assert m._git_ok(root, 'add', '-A') and m._git_ok(root, 'commit', '-q', '-m', name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root/'packets', root/'contracts', m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f'{name}: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else f'RED[{len(errors)}] ' + ' || '.join(errors)))
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run('date-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": ""'))
+run('date-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": 20260803'))
+run('date-impossible-calendar', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": "2026-02-31"'))
+run('date-escaped-final-newline', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": "2026-08-03\\n"'))
+run('branch-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run('branch-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": []'))
+run('branch-no-such', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": "no-such"'))
+run('tip-empty', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": ""',t))
+run('tip-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": null',t))
+run('tip-uppercase', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "([0-9a-f]{40})"',lambda x:'"oracle_tip": "'+x.group(1).upper()+'"',t))
+run('tip-off-branch', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"',f'"oracle_tip": "{o}"',t))
+run('plan-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": ""'))
+run('plan-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": {}'))
+run('plan-absolute', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "/tmp/plan.md"'))
+run('plan-missing', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "no-such.md"'))
+run('plan-normalizing-dotdot', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "contracts/../plan-fixture.md"'))
+run('record-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run('row-semantic-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | changed |'), commit=True)
+run('row-trailing-space-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | the row |   '), commit=True)
+run('realized-map-on-superseded', lambda t,o:t+'\n```json\n{"realized_map":{"C1":"x","C2":"y"}}\n```\n')
+run('manifest-anchor', remove_packet=False)
+run('header-union-only', remove_packet=False, packet_mut=lambda t:t.replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"','"prose:header-c1"').replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", ','"prose:header-c2", ')+'\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1/C2\n')
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+(root/'packets'/'ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md').write_text('# old\n\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n',encoding='utf-8')
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print(f'grandfathered-header-union-only: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)) + f' (grandfathered={stats["grandfathered"]})')
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR,forbid_reopened=True)
+print('forbid-reopened-with-superseded: '+('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)))
+print('summary-superseded: '+repr(stats['superseded']))
+tmp.cleanup()

+import importlib.util
+from pathlib import Path
+
+LINTER = Path('/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py')
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('d8lint', LINTER)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+def run(name, draft_mut=lambda t, off: t, packet_mut=None, remove_packet=True, commit=False):
+    fx = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fx is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fx
+    draft.write_text(draft_mut(text, off), encoding='utf-8')
+    if remove_packet:
+        packet.unlink()
+    elif packet_mut:
+        packet.write_text(packet_mut(packet.read_text(encoding='utf-8')), encoding='utf-8')
+    if commit:
+        assert m._git_ok(root, 'add', '-A') and m._git_ok(root, 'commit', '-q', '-m', name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root/'packets', root/'contracts', m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f'{name}: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else f'RED[{len(errors)}] ' + ' || '.join(errors)))
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run('date-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": ""'))
+run('date-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": 20260803'))
+run('date-impossible-calendar', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": "2026-02-31"'))
+run('date-escaped-final-newline', lambda t,o:t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"','"date": "2026-08-03\\n"'))
+run('branch-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run('branch-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": []'))
+run('branch-no-such', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"','"oracle_branch": "no-such"'))
+run('tip-empty', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": ""',t))
+run('tip-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"','"oracle_tip": null',t))
+run('tip-uppercase', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "([0-9a-f]{40})"',lambda x:'"oracle_tip": "'+x.group(1).upper()+'"',t))
+run('tip-off-branch', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"',f'"oracle_tip": "{o}"',t))
+run('plan-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": ""'))
+run('plan-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": {}'))
+run('plan-absolute', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "/tmp/plan.md"'))
+run('plan-missing', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "no-such.md"'))
+run('plan-normalizing-dotdot', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "contracts/../plan-fixture.md"'))
+run('record-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run('row-semantic-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | changed |'), commit=True)
+run('row-trailing-space-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | the row |   '), commit=True)
+run('row-one-space-appended', lambda t,o:t+'\n | C3 | accidentally appended |\n', commit=True)
+run('realized-map-on-superseded', lambda t,o:t+'\n```json\n{"realized_map":{"C1":"x","C2":"y"}}\n```\n')
+run('manifest-anchor', remove_packet=False)
+run('header-union-only', remove_packet=False, packet_mut=lambda t:t.replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"','"prose:header-c1"').replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", ','"prose:header-c2", ')+'\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1/C2\n')
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+(root/'packets'/'ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md').write_text('# old\n\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n',encoding='utf-8')
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print(f'grandfathered-header-union-only: ' + ('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)) + f' (grandfathered={stats["grandfathered"]})')
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR,forbid_reopened=True)
+print('forbid-reopened-with-superseded: '+('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)))
+print('summary-superseded: '+repr(stats['superseded']))
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+record_head='\n```json\n{"superseded"'
+draft.write_text(text.replace('"status": "superseded"','"status": "ratified"').split(record_head)[0]+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
+assert m._git_ok(root,'add','-A') and m._git_ok(root,'commit','-q','-m','accidental-terminal-exit')
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print('terminal-exit-to-ratified: '+('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors))+' summary='+repr(stats['superseded']))
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink(); draft.unlink()
+errors,stats=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print('archived-draft-deleted: '+('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors))+f' drafts={stats["drafts"]} summary='+repr(stats['superseded']))
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+fx=m.build_superseded_fixture(); assert fx is not None
+tmp,root,draft,packet,text,off=fx; packet.unlink()
+draft.write_text(text+'\n> | ID | Rule |\n> |---|---|\n> | C3 | deliberately blockquoted concealed row |\n',encoding='utf-8')
+assert m._git_ok(root,'add','-A') and m._git_ok(root,'commit','-q','-m','blockquote-concealment')
+errors,_=m.lint(root/'packets',root/'contracts',m.ADR_DIR)
+print('blockquote-concealed-row: '+('GREEN' if not errors else 'RED '+ ' || '.join(errors)))
+tmp.cleanup()

+run('tip-off-branch', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"',f'"oracle_tip": "{o}"',t))
+run('plan-empty', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": ""'))
+run('plan-wrong-type', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": {}'))
+run('plan-absolute', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "/tmp/plan.md"'))
+run('plan-missing', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "no-such.md"'))
+run('plan-normalizing-dotdot', lambda t,o:t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"','"plan": "contracts/../plan-fixture.md"'))
+run('record-wrong-type', lambda t,o:__import__('re').sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run('row-semantic-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | changed |'), commit=True)
+run('row-trailing-space-edit', lambda t,o:t.replace('| C1 | the row |','| C1 | the row |   '), commit=True)
+run('row-one-space-appended', lambda t,o:t+'\n | C3 | accidentally appended |\n', commit=True)
+run('realized-map-on-superseded', lambda t,o:t+'\n```json\n{"realized_map":{"C1":"x","C2":"y"}}\n```\n')
+run('manifest-anchor', remove_packet=False)
+run('header-union-only', remove_packet=False, packet_mut=lambda t:t.replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"','"prose:header-c1"').replace('"contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", ','"prose:header-c2", ')+'\nContract anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1/C2\n')
